Do NOT apply for factual lookups, calculations, or precise technical how-to requests. ([re-incarnate-1/skills](https://github.com/re-incarnate-1/skills/tree/HEAD/examined-thinking))
- **examiner**: Use this skill at the start of every coding session before writing new code. - **example-skill**: REPLACE THIS. A skill auto-surfaces purely on this text, and the listing TRUNCATES it at 1,536 chars (tail first) inside a shared budget — so front-load and stay ≤1,000 chars. Formula: &lt;domain/contract in one clause&gt; — &lt;what it carries: the 3–5 specifics an agent can't guess&gt;. Load when &lt;the tasks it covers&gt;.
